# Domain 23 — Current Program State

## Executive Summary

Domain 23 Current Program State сертифицирован. Его mission — хранить volatile snapshot текущей operational reality: phase, blocker, owner resolution, maturity context, current safe next action and current stop reason. Идеальный кандидат совпадает с текущим доменом. CPS не является Authority, OMP, roadmap, truth source, Runtime, Planner or maturity scorer. Архитектура не меняется; отсутствующий certification evidence восстановлен.

## Mission Discovery

Current Program State существует, чтобы будущая engineering session могла продолжить работу без потери позиции. Фундаментальная проблема — V7 нуждается в текущей карте "где мы сейчас", но эта карта не должна становиться разрешением, roadmap authority или вторым источником истины.

Отдельный домен оправдан, потому что Production Maturity хранит maturity truth, OMP выбирает next mission, Engineering Reports сохраняют evidence, а CPS хранит volatile current operational state.

## Mission Success Model

Success: CPS accurately exposes current phase, blocker, owner, terminal root cause, required resolution, maturity context and safe next action without granting permission.

Failure: CPS becomes stale, approves Runtime apply, expands Authority, creates backlog/roadmap, recalculates Production Maturity, hides blockers, or replaces OMP.

Inputs: Production Maturity decision, OMP decision, certification results, Engineering Reports, current blocker, current transition state.

Outputs: current product reality, current active target, current transition state, current blockers, current readiness context.

Producers: Production Maturity, OMP, certification owners, Engineering Reports.

Consumers: OMP, Product Observation, dashboard/read models, human handoff.

| R1 / R2 / R3 / R4 | COMPLETE | operational state, incident command visibility, owner mapping, no duplicate truth. |
| Function Graph / implementation / tests | COMPLETE | admin/read-model current state and dashboard consumers; no mutation authority required. |

## V7 Coverage Ledger

`docs/programs/V7_CURRENT_PROGRAM_STATE.md` defines CPS as volatile consumer of Production Maturity outputs and explicitly forbids it from approving Runtime apply, expanding authority, enabling automation, moving users, changing routing, or creating backlog/roadmap/owner/planner/campaign/truth source.

## Research Coverage Ledger

External engineering principles support live operational status as a situational-awareness layer. They do not support turning current status into action authority. This maps directly to CPS as volatile visibility, not control plane.

## Implementation Coverage Ledger

Implementation reality shows current-state-like data consumed by admin/operator read models, dashboards, execution readiness views and OMP visibility. These nodes are read-only/advisory or operator visibility; mutation remains owned elsewhere.

## Second-Pass Review

No source allows CPS to grant authority or create production mutation. Potential contradiction — CPS contains "required action" text — resolves as operator-facing next-step visibility, not execution permission.

Ideal CPS is a volatile current-state mirror that lets humans and OMP resume correctly while preserving canonical owner boundaries.

## Ideal Domain Candidate

Current Program State is the volatile operational state snapshot for V7. It records current phase, blocker, owner resolution, maturity/readiness context and safe next step, then exposes that state to OMP and operator surfaces without becoming authority.

## Current Domain Discovery

Current Domain 23 matches this candidate and explicitly prohibits authority, automation, routing, backlog, roadmap, owner, planner, campaign or truth-source behavior.

## Trusted Knowledge

- CPS is volatile operational state.
- CPS is not authority, Runtime, OMP, roadmap, truth source or maturity scorer.
- CPS updates only when volatile state changes.
- Existing owners are sufficient.

# Domain 24 — OMP

## Executive Summary

Domain 24 OMP сертифицирован. Его mission — быть single permanent operating program for V7: consume evidence/current state/maturity, route work to existing owners, continue after blockers, and prevent duplicate roadmaps. Идеальный кандидат совпадает с текущим доменом. OMP is not Runtime, Planner, Authority, Truth Source or implementation shortcut. Архитектура не меняется; corpus evidence восстановлен.

## Mission Discovery

OMP существует, чтобы V7 не останавливалась на отчете, blocker, partial implementation or stale roadmap. Фундаментальная проблема — автономная production platform needs a program owner that converts evidence, blockers, maturity gaps and authority boundaries into the next governed mission.

Отдельный домен оправдан: CPS shows current position, Production Maturity classifies maturity, owners execute their contracts, but OMP determines continuation and routes work without replacing owners.

## Mission Success Model

Success: every blocker becomes classified work, every capability gap routes to an existing owner, duplicate roadmaps are rejected, and execution continues until PASS/HOLD/BLOCKED with root resolution or canonical impossibility.

Failure: OMP becomes a second Runtime/Planner/Authority, invents parallel roadmaps, stops at reports, or treats documentation as capability.

Inputs: CPS, Production Maturity, Engineering Reports, Gap Register/backlog, certifications, owner-resolution outputs.

Outputs: next mission, owner routing, maturity/certification order, capability continuation state.

Producers: Engineering Reports, Production Maturity, CPS, certification owners, implementation owners.

Consumers: Codex execution, owners, operator/handoff, CPS, Production Maturity.

## V7 Coverage Ledger

ADR-V7-OMP-PRODUCTION-PROGRAM and `OPERATIONAL_MATURITY_PROGRAM.md` define OMP as the single execution program. MASTER handoff says OMP owns maturity order, certification, capability progression and engineering discipline, while forbidding duplicate OMP or new architecture.

## Research Coverage Ledger

World research supports an operating program that routes evidence-backed work, preserves ownership, classifies blockers, and drives maturity. It does not support creating a second control plane or bypassing safety owners.

## Implementation Coverage Ledger

Function Graph shows OMP-adjacent implementation through governed canary cycles, admin read models, acceleration inventory, evidence-based improvement, workflow views and tests. Some nodes are read-only/advisory; production mutation remains under specialized owners.

## Second-Pass Review

No contradiction found: OMP can schedule/rout work and certify progression, but cannot directly mutate production or bypass Authority/Runtime.

## Ideal Domain Discovery

Ideal OMP is the program-level continuation owner: it consumes evidence, chooses legal next work, routes to existing owners, tracks maturity and prevents dead-end reports.

## Ideal Domain Candidate

OMP is V7's permanent production operating program. It governs continuation, certification order, owner routing and capability progression while preserving existing owner boundaries.

## Current Domain Discovery

Current Domain 24 matches: OMP is coordination/continuation, not a new architecture or production mutation owner.

Separate domain justified. No other domain owns program continuation across all owners. Merging into CPS, Production Maturity, Runtime or Authority would either lose continuation or violate ownership boundaries.

## Trusted Knowledge

- OMP is the single permanent execution/continuation program.
- OMP routes work; it does not replace owners.

- Existing OMP owner is sufficient.

# Domain 25 — Engineering Automation

## Executive Summary

Domain 25 Engineering Automation сертифицирован. Его mission — turn repeated manual engineering actions and workflows into owner-backed governed automation or explicit terminal classifications. Идеальный кандидат совпадает с текущим доменом. Engineering Automation improves engineering execution but cannot bypass production safety, Authority, Runtime, Verification, Rollback or OMP. Архитектура не меняется; corpus evidence восстановлен.

## Mission Discovery

Engineering Automation существует, чтобы V7 не зависела от постоянной ручной orchestration by Codex/operator for repeated, valuable, safety-bounded engineering workflows. Проблема не в одной команде, а в repeated manual work that should become an owned, testable pipeline or be classified as intentionally manual/not cost-effective.

Отдельный домен оправдан: OMP routes missions, but Engineering Automation specifically owns automation debt, workflow debt, pipeline candidates and command minimization.

## Mission Success Model

Success: repeated manual work is classified; valuable repeatable workflows become governed pipelines under existing owners; automation is suspendable; safety owners remain intact.

Failure: manual workflows remain unexplained, automation becomes personal script/Codex-only toil, or automation bypasses production gates.

Inputs: manual actions, repeated workflows, Engineering Reports, OMP missions, debt metrics, owner mapping.

Outputs: automation candidates, pipeline candidates, automation debt classification, workflow debt classification, implemented/certified pipelines when justified.

Producers: OMP, Codex execution reports, engineering workflows, Function Graph/readiness tools.

Consumers: OMP, Continuous Self Evolution, CPS, engineering owners.

Controlled Production Certification Program and Master Handoff define manual action/workflow audit, automation debt, workflow debt, pipeline candidates and command minimization. CPS exposes automation and workflow debt deltas.

## Research Coverage Ledger

World research supports automation of repeated safe operations, toil reduction, runbook-to-pipeline evolution and automation guardrails. It rejects unmanaged automation that bypasses safety or human policy boundaries.

## Implementation Coverage Ledger

Function Graph shows workflow/readiness and advisory implementation such as `execution_workflow_items`, `p2_7_workflow_consistency`, `build_rt2_s6_evidence_based_continuous_improvement`, and tests around candidate workflow bridge. Several workflow nodes are read-only or partially dormant, which is acceptable because this domain defines engineering evolution, not production mutation.

## Second-Pass Review

No contradiction found. Implementation is not expected to automate every workflow yet; the domain is correct if it classifies debt and routes justified automation through existing owners.

## Ideal Domain Discovery

Ideal Engineering Automation tracks manual toil, investigates why it exists, classifies debt, and converts repeatable safe workflows into governed owner-backed pipelines.

## Ideal Domain Candidate

Engineering Automation is the engineering-system improvement domain that reduces repeated manual work and orchestration through evidence-backed automation candidates and pipeline candidates, while preserving all production safety owners.

## Current Domain Discovery

Current Domain 25 matches: it owns Automation Audit, Workflow Audit, automation/workflow debt, pipeline candidates, analyzer backtesting and command minimization, while excluding production mutation and safety bypass.

- Workflow debt is separate from single manual action debt.
- Engineering Automation must use existing owners.
- Automation must be suspendable and safety-bounded.

# Domain 26 — Continuous Self Evolution

## Executive Summary

Domain 26 Continuous Self Evolution сертифицирован. Его mission — ensure every real terminal outcome feeds future capability, automation, workflow orchestration, maturity, learning and next missions. Идеальный кандидат совпадает с текущим доменом. It is a closed improvement loop, not a new Runtime, Planner, Authority, OMP, truth source or self-modifying production system. Архитектура не меняется; corpus evidence восстановлен.

## Mission Discovery

Continuous Self Evolution существует, чтобы V7 не завершалась отчетом, certification result or manual workflow. Фундаментальная проблема — every real process must create evidence, every evidence must either improve capability or classify a boundary, and repeated manual work must become a safe improvement candidate or be intentionally classified.

Отдельный домен оправдан: Learning captures outcome evidence, OMP routes next mission, Engineering Automation reduces workflow toil, Production Maturity consumes maturity evidence. Continuous Self Evolution defines the closed loop tying these outcomes into ongoing system improvement.

## Mission Success Model

Success: terminal outcomes feed learning/maturity/OMP/automation; automation and workflow debt are classified; future missions become safer and clearer; no process terminates into unexplained TODO.

Failure: reports become dead ends, blockers do not become missions, repeated workflows remain unexplained, or "self evolution" bypasses human policy, authority or safety owners.

Inputs: terminal outcomes, learning records, maturity decisions, engineering reports, automation/workflow debt, OMP mission results.

Outputs: next mission, capability evolution evidence, automation evolution evidence, workflow evolution evidence, classified boundaries.

Producers: Learning, OMP, Production Maturity, Engineering Automation, certification programs.

Consumers: OMP, CPS, Product/Engineering owners, future domain/program work.

## Source Coverage Ledger

| Source class | Coverage | Evidence used |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Knowledge Consolidation | COMPLETE | Domain 26 definition, purpose, success/failure criteria. |
| OMP / AOS / SYSTEM_MAP | COMPLETE | final autonomous target and continuous process. |
| CPS / Production Maturity | COMPLETE | current capability and maturity consumption. |
| Canonical Reference / Product Spec / Reports | COMPLETE | "gets better forever" and continuous process evidence. |
| R1 / R2 / R3 / R4 | COMPLETE | continuous improvement, learning systems, operational maturity loops. |
| Function Graph / implementation / tests | COMPLETE | advisory continuous-improvement read models and learning/maturity consumers. |

## V7 Coverage Ledger

AOS defines the final autonomous target including learning from every terminal outcome, updating current state/maturity, creating missions from gaps, classifying repeated manual actions/workflows, and improving the engineering system. Master Handoff defines the continuous process from Reality to Evidence to Capability to Authority to Production to Certification to Automation/Workflow Audit to Next Mission.

## Research Coverage Ledger

World research supports continuous improvement loops, post-incident learning, maturity feedback, progressive automation, and operational excellence. It does not support autonomous architecture rewrite or safety bypass without evidence.

## Implementation Coverage Ledger

Function Graph shows supporting advisory/read-model paths such as `build_rt2_s6_evidence_based_continuous_improvement`, maturity/readiness models, learning/trust/evidence inventories and workflow views. These are consistent with self-evolution as governed evidence-driven improvement, not autonomous production mutation.

## Second-Pass Review

No contradiction found. The phrase "self evolution" could imply self-modifying system behavior, but V7 evidence resolves it as evidence-driven owner-routed improvement under OMP and existing owners.

## Ideal Domain Discovery

Ideal Continuous Self Evolution is the closed-loop system improvement domain: outcomes become evidence, evidence becomes capability/maturity/automation/workflow decisions, and decisions become future missions.

## Ideal Domain Candidate

Continuous Self Evolution is the domain that makes V7 improve after every real cycle by connecting Learning, OMP, Production Maturity and Engineering Automation into a single evidence-driven improvement loop.

## Current Domain Discovery

Current Domain 26 matches: it defines capability evolution, automation evolution and workflow evolution as one continuous improvement loop while excluding policy approval, new owners and autonomy without evidence.

## Structural Comparison

| Boundary | No owner bypass or self-authority. | Same. | MATCH |
| Inputs | terminal outcomes and debt. | Same. | MATCH |
| Outputs | next missions and improved capability/workflow. | Same. | MATCH |

## Architectural Equivalence

`EQUIVALENT`

Separate domain justified. It cannot merge into Learning because Learning is outcome evidence, not whole-system evolution. It cannot merge into OMP because OMP routes missions, while self-evolution defines the improvement loop. It cannot merge into Engineering Automation because capability evolution is broader than automation.

## Trusted Knowledge

- V7 improvement must be evidence-driven.
- No terminal outcome should disappear silently.
- Self-evolution does not grant authority or create new owners.
- Continuous improvement connects capability, automation and workflow evolution.
